2026-11-03T07:43:10.3834141Z   UV_CACHE_DIR: /home/runner/work/_temp/setup-uv-cache
2026-11-03T07:43:10.3841925Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve PP4 round 26 with the Prefill MegaMoE N16/N32 swap tail behind its all-GPU gate

Round 26 computes the last pool block of an expert, when it holds 1-32
valid rows, as a swap-AB WGMMA with N16 or N32 instead of padding it to
64 rows. Weights become the A operand and tokens the B operand. The
change is limited to Prefill EP1 with T7988, E128, H4096 and I1536. It
keeps the pool, TMA boxes, scheduler order, carried-scale order,
per-128 FC1 quantization and combine. Commits cae926c, 271e9e2 and
13c5e1b by Codex hold the kernel, the CPU proof and the in-job gate.

- CPU: all 36 saved distributions and every remainder match in
  coordinates, scales, gate/up, swizzle and stage/arrival order. An
  independent FP32 NumPy reference at the real K agrees to 3.6e-7.
  Tensor Core accumulation order cannot be proven on a CPU.
- CUDA 13.4 sm_90a: no spill and no C75xx. All 24 existing configurations
  without the flag are SASS-identical. The candidate adds about 5.4% more
  WGMMA issues and about 40% more code. Whether it is faster is unmeasured.
  The image's CUDA is 13.0.88, so this build is the first real compile and
  load.
- Historical fixture: 58 experts and 2,576 padding rows, 0.097 TFLOP per
  layer of discarded MMA work removed.

The gate compares final outputs, FC1 FP8 and scales, and FC2 BF16
partials byte for byte on all twelve Prefill GPUs. It runs 1200
consecutive calls and times both orders in one process. It adopts only
if every GPU is bit-identical and the full MegaMoE median improves by
max(5 us, 0.5%) in both orders. Anything else serves the current
4STAGE/LEAN/SPLIT path.

Changes made for this push:
- The gate block now runs right after pair/prepack and before the Decode
  greedy gate. Round 25 reached the end of the greedy gate at about 236 s
  (job start 12:56:36, greedy gate 13:00:04 plus 28 s). That is past
  this gate's 235 s limit, so placed after the greedy gate it would always
  have skipped. The greedy gate reserves all eight g4 devices, so the two
  cannot overlap. With this order the greedy gate skips on its own
  budget. The unfused path is index-identical and round 25 showed no
  score effect, so the swap tail stays the only main factor. Expected end
  is about 900-970 s of the 1020 s window: 208 s, plus at most 125 s,
  plus 566-634 s after the gates in rounds 25 and 21.
- The DeepGEMM host prints one line per process when the swap tail is
  enabled. The sbatch keeps the counted lines from prefill-0..2.log in
  results/prefill-megamoe-swap-tail-dispatch.txt next to the score.
  Round 22's gate adopted a kernel that serving never ran, so adoption
  alone is not evidence.
- configs: prefill.megamoe_swap_tail_gate is true. The image pin stays at
  a001f901, and this push builds a fresh image.

CI-style (project CPython 3.13, default TMPDIR).
